Its p_model is the board's own de-vigged consensus for this exact selection, republished unchanged by analyze()'s PROJECT phase under the identity map -- zero fitted parameters, no recalibration intercept, no Platt, no isotonic refit (docs/PREREG_CALIBRATED_PROBABILITY.md §2).
